<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Eelektrik</strong><br><em>From Black Bolt</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Ability: Dynamotor<br>Once during your turn, you may attach a Basic [L] Energy card from your discard pile to 1 of your Benched Pokémon.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">[L][L][C] Electric Ball: 50 damage.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><em>NOTE: Despite being effectively functionally identical to </em><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/NVI/40" target="_blank"><em>Eelektrik NVI 40</em></a><em>, it has been ruled that this is </em><strong><em>not </em></strong><em>a 1:1 reprint because of the change in templating for the effect of Dynamotor, which now specifically includes the word “Basic.” The Noble Victories Eelektrik will </em><strong><em>not </em></strong><em>be legal in the Standard format following this card’s release.</em></p>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Ignition Energy</strong><br>From White Flare</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">If this card is attached to 1 of your Pokémon, discard it at the end of the turn.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">As long as this card is attached to a Pokémon, it provides [C] Energy.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">If this card is attached to an Evolution Pokémon, this card provides [C][C][C] Energy instead.</p>
